Federal Office for Information Security 

90 

2 Test Cases Function Modules 

## **Test Case ID: TC-PAD-FI-APP-002** 

- _Preconditions_ **•** The IUT is in operation, required modules are loaded. 

   - The camera system used in the specific application scenario is connected. 

   - The IUT implements an interface for conformance testing where a single image can be captured and the computed PAD values are returned. 

   - The PAD functionality of the IUT is activated for performing the test case. 

   - The IUT is able to return the results for every captured image via the test interface. 

   - The IUT is able to return the results for the conducted PAD. 

   - The following test resources are necessary: 

      - At least three different biometric subjects SHALL available to perform bona fide presentati ons. 

      - Four different artefacts of each of the eight artefact classes SHALL be created, so that a total of 32 artefacts are obtained. These artefacts SHOULD be usually constructed before testing begins. For every testrun new artefacts SHOULD be constructed. 

      - Class 1: Masks created from casted silicon, big (size of a face) and small (smaller than a normal face), in different thicknesses: 3-D presentation. 

      - Class 2: Masks created from latex in different thicknesses and styles: 3-D presentation. 

      - Class 3: Masks printed on paper in different thicknesses and styles: 3-D presentation, the pho tograph of the biometric subject is printed on paper but presented in a 3-D manner (used as a mask covering the whole face or only parts of it). 

      - Class 4: Masks printed with 3D-printer in different thicknesses and styles: 3-D presentation. 

      - Class 5: Photographs displayed on electronic devices, e.g. phones, tablets, etc.: 2-D presentati on. 

      - Class 6: Videos displayed on electronic devices, e.g. phones, tablets, etc.: 2-D presentation. 

      - Class 7: Faces printed on paper with different thicknesses and structures of paper and diffe rent structures of printing (colouring, etc.): 2-D presentation, a photograph of the biometric subject is presented to the system as a flat paper copy. 

      - Faces printed on fabrics with different thicknesses and structures of the fabrics and different methods of printing (flock print, silk screening, etc.): 2-D or 3-D presentation, a photograph of the biometric subject is presented to the system on fabrics (e.g. on a t-shirt). A 3-D presen tation can materialise if pieces of clothing are used by the biometric subject. 

      - For the selection of electronic devices for the artefact classes 5 and 6: At least three different devices from three different manufacturers are used. 

      - For the selection of electronic devices for the artefact classes 5 and 6: No device is older than five years (calculated from the date of release in Germany). 

      - For the selection of electronic devices for the artefact classes 5 and 6: At least one smartphone and one tablet is used. 

_CTS Mode_ 

- interactive 

- no provision of pre-defined input data 

- `HTTP method: GET` 

- test case variants: 

```
/TR03122/TC-PAD-FI-APP-002/1
```

**Table 2.131** TC-PAD-FI-APP-002 Test Case Variants 

_Description_ 

**Step Description / Expected Result** 

Federal Office for Information Security 

91 

2 Test Cases Function Modules 

- **Test Case ID: TC-PAD-FI-APP-002** 1 _Description:_ **•** Initiate test case by calling the IUT via the test interface. **•** Perform a facial acquisition using either a test subject (bona-fide) or a PAI presentation. If a PAI presentation is performed, the presentations are per formed depending on the PAI by holding the artefact manually into the ca mera, wearing the artefact as a mask or by using a special construction in the IUTs capture area. 

- **•** The response returned by the IUT via the test interface is evaluated. _Expected Result:_ **•** The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

- **•** The response contains the encoded facial image (bio:FaceAcquisition) **•** If a bona-fide presentation was performed: All acceptance conditions for the facial image acquisition are satisfied and achieved. 

- **•** The IUT's PAD returns its detection results in the form of a normalized PAD score in the range [0,1] where 0 indicates bona fide and 1 presentation attack to the calling application. 

- **•** If a bona-fide presentation was performed: The PAD score returned by the IUT indicates a bona fide face presentation. 

- **•** If a presentation using a PAI was performed: The PAD score returned by the IUT indicates a presentation attack. 

- **•** If a bona-fide presentation was performed: No PAD alarm is shown to the operator. 

- **•** If a PAD presentation was performed: A PAD alarm is shown to the opera tor. 

- _Evaluation_ **Evaluation Step Description / Evaluation Result** 1 _Description:_ **•** For each of the three biometric subjects the test case is repeated 20 times re sulting in a total of 60 bona-fide presentations. 

- **•** For each of the PAI the test case is repeated 10 times resulting in a total of 320 PAI presentations. 

- **•** In each step slight alterations in the presentation are allowed (e.g. covering of integrated lights, wearing additional accessories like glasses). 

- _Expected Result:_ **•** An artefact is considered as detected, if the device shows a PAD alarm in six out of ten attempts. If the IUT (repeatable) does not accept a presentation and delivers the result "no face found" (or alike), the presentation is counted as "detected". 

- **•** A device failed the total test, if a single artefact class never triggered a PAD alarm. 

   - A device failed the total test, if nine or more artefacts in total were not de tected as stated above. 

   - A device failed the total test, if more than three of the bona fide facial images are not recognized as bona fide. Failures of detection from the other test steps do not count for this requirement. Note that during certification a more relaxed false-alarm-rate is tested than specified within the respec tive Function Module. The requirement of the rate specified in the respec tive Function Module is tested on operational collected data only. 

**Table 2.130** Test Case ID: TC-PAD-FI-APP-002

2 Test Cases Function Modules 

## **Test Case ID: TC-PAD-FP-APP-002** 

- _Preconditions_ **•** The IUT is in operation, required modules are loaded. 

   - The fingerprint scanner used in the specific application scenario is connected 

   - The IUT implements an interface for conformance testing where a fingerprints can be captured and the computed PAD values are returned. 

   - The PAD functionality of the IUT is activated for performing the test case. 

   - The IUT is able to return the results for every captured finger via the test interface. 

   - The IUT is able to return the results for the conducted PAD. 

   - The following test resources are necessary: 

      - At least three different biometric subjects (with generally good fingerprints) SHALL available to perform bona fide presentations. 

      - Three different artefacts of each of the seven artefact classes SHALL be created, so that a total of 21 artefacts are obtained. The following artefacts classes (fingertips in different thicknesses and/or massive fingers) SHALL be used during test cases 

      - Class 1: Artefacts created from different kinds of silicon, in different colouring. 

      - Class 2: Artefacts created from different kinds of latex, in different colouring. 

      - Class 3: Artefacts created from different kinds of gelatine, in different colouring. 

      - Class 4: Artefacts created from different kinds of wood glue, in different colouring. 

      - Class 5: Artefacts created from different kinds of window painting, in different colouring. 

      - Class 6: Artefacts created from different kinds of paper, in different colouring. 

      - Class 7:Artefacts created from different kinds of latex with window painting (using the follo wing ratios: 2:1, 1:1, 1:2), in different colouring. 

      - The materials of the different artefact classes SHALL NOT be mixed. Different kinds of each artefact class MAY be created adding glycerol (0.5 ml per gramm of artefact class material ma ximum) and/or graphite (5 knife-point per gramm of artefact class material maximum). For each testrun, new artefacts MAY be created. 

      - Artefacts SHALL provide a NFIQ-2.2-score of at least 30 (or an equivalent score with other NFIQ versions). 

_CTS Mode_ 

- interactive 

- no provision of pre-defined input data 

- `HTTP method: GET` 

- test case variants: 

```
/TR03122/TC-PAD-FP-APP-002/1
```

**Table 2.134** TC-PAD-FP-APP-002 Test Case Variants 

_Description_ 

|**Step**|**Description / Expected Result**|
|---|---|
|1|_Description:_|



- Initiate test case by calling the IUT via the test interface. 

- Perform a fingerprint acquisition using either a test subject (bona-fide) or a PAI. 

- If a PAI is performed exactly one finger of exactly one hand SHOULD be equipped with the artefact per fingerprint acquisition 

- The response returned by the IUT via the test interface is evaluated. 

Federal Office for Information Security 

94 

2 Test Cases Function Modules 

## **Test Case ID: TC-PAD-FP-APP-002** 

_Expected Result:_ 

   - The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

   - The response contains the encoded fingerprint image (bio:FingerAcquisiti on) 

   - **•** If a bona-fide presentation was performed: All acceptance conditions for the fingerprint image acquisition are satisfied and achieved. 

   - The IUT's PAD returns its detection results in the form of a normalized PAD score in the range [0,1] where 0 indicates bona fide and 1 presentation attack to the calling application. Also, an overall PAD score is returned. 

   - If a bona-fide presentation was performed: The PAD score returned by the IUT indicates a bona fide presentation. 

   - If a PAI presentation was performed: The PAD score returned by the IUT in dicates a presentation attack. 

   - If a bona-fide presentation was performed: No PAD alarm is shown to the operator. 

   - If a PAD presentation was performed: A PAD alarm is shown to the opera tor. 

   - The individual PAD scores returned by the IUT indicate a presentation at tack for fingers with artefacts and bona fide for fingers without artefacts. 

- Every finger originating from an artefact is correctly assigned to its corre sponding finger code. (Abstract example: If there is a hand with four bona fide fingers and one finger originating from an artefact, then the PAD score stating it is a presentation attack is assigned to the corresponding finger. Concrete example: If there is a hand with only the index finger coming from an artefact, then the PAD score stating it is a presentation attack is assigned to the index finger.) 

- _Evaluation_ **Evaluation Step Description / Evaluation Result** 1 _Description:_ **•** For each of the three biometric subjects the test case is repeated 20 times re sulting in a total of 60 bona-fide presentations. 

- **•** For each of the PAI the test case is repeated 10 times resulting in a total of 210 PAI presentations. 

- **•** In each step where a PAI is used a rotation principle should be in place that specifies that the equipment of a finger with an artefact changes or rolls in each step. This is done to ensure that not always the same finger is equipped with an artefact for each fingerprint acquisition. Doing this every artefact SHOULD be present for at least three different fingers of at least one hand. 

- _Expected Result:_ 

   - An artefact is considered as detected, if the device shows a PAD alarm in at least six out of ten attempts. 

   - A device failed the total test, if a single artefact class never triggered a PAD alarm. 

   - A device failed the total test, if four or more artefacts in total were not de tected as stated above. 

   - A device failed the total test, if within the bona fide part of the test more than three of the bona fide presentations are not recognized as bona fide. Failures of detection from the PAI part of the test do not count for this re quirement. Note that during certification a more relaxed false-alarm-rate is tested than specified within the respective Function Module. The requi rement of the rate specified in the respective Function Module is tested on operational collected data only 

**Table 2.133** Test Case ID: TC-PAD-FP-APP-002

## 2.6 Test Cases Compression 

## 2.6.1 Test Cases FM COM-FI-BCL 

## **Test Case ID: TC-COM-FI-BCL-001** 

- _Scope_ Examine the correctness of the compression of live images used within the context of border con trol. 

- _Preconditions_ **•** The IUT is in operation, required modules are loaded. **•** As necessary test resources: a test database containing multiple compressed and uncompressed (including losslessly compressed) facial images. The provided formats are BMP, PNG, JPEG and JPEG2000. 

- **•** The IUT implements an interface for conformance testing where a single image can be provided and the according biometric container including the image data is returned. 

- **•** The following conditions apply regarding the database: **•** The image data that is sent to the IUT is of the following biometric type: FACE. **•** The groundtruth database contains compliant images w.r.t. the required quality values. **•** Note: Images in BMP, PNG and JPEG2000 formats are considered uncompressed or lossless compressed. 

- **•** Note: Images in JPEG format are considered lossy compressed. 

- _CTS Mode_ **•** automatic **•** The image data that is sent to the IUT may have the following formats: **•** BMP **•** PNG **•** JPEG **•** JPEG2000 

- **•** `HTTP method: POST` **•** test case variants: 

```
/TR03122/TC-COM-FI-BCL-001/1
```

**Table 2.136** TC-COM-FI-BCL-001 Test Case Variants 

|_Description_|**Step**|**Description / Expected Result**|
|---|---|---|
||1|_Description:_|



- The IUT is called via the conformance test interface and an image from the database is provided. The image meets the properties described in the pre conditions. 

- Extraction of the compressed facial image file from the biometric container. 

Federal Office for Information Security 

96 

2 Test Cases Function Modules 

## **Test Case ID: TC-COM-FI-BCL-001** 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file bcl6v1.xsd. 

- If the provided image has a different format than JPEG: The resulting image is compressed using JPEG2000 (according to ISO/IEC 15444-1) or JPEG (ac cording to ISO/IEC 10918-1) compression standard. 

- If the response from the IUT contains an image: The image is compressed using the compression standard referenced in the respective `bio:Binary Record/@type` . 

- If the provided image has a different format than JPEG: The actual file size of the compressed image is below 375 kB. 

- If the response from the IUT contains an image: The compression level is at most 20:1. 

- If the response from the IUT contains an image: The image resolution of the returned image is the same as that of the input image. 

- If the provided image has JPEG format: Since multiple lossy compression is not allowed, either an error code that the acceptance conditions are not met is returned, or no additional processing is performed on the input image (i. e. the returned image is bitwise identical to the provided image). 

|_Evaluation_|**Evaluation Step**|**Description / Evaluation Result**|
|---|---|---|
||1|_Description:_|
|||**•**<br>Examine the response returned by the IUT via the test interface.|
|||_Expected Result:_|



- Overall, less then 0 percent of the requested images are not handled as ex pected. 

**Table 2.135** Test Case ID: TC-COM-FI-BCL-001

2 

_Description:_ 

- Position the test subject looking frontally into the camera at a distance of 70 cm from the camera system. This distance is to be understood as the length of the optical path. 

- Position the test subject with a body height of 170 cm in front of the camera system. Capture a facial image. 

- Calculate the frontal obstruction angle _¯_ 1 at the nasion of the biometric subject looking at the device. The frontal obstruction angle is calculated using the outermost edges of the device in the area 20 cm below and above the height of the "Frankfurt Horizon" of the test subject. 

- Calculate the backward obstruction angle _¯_ 2 at the nasion of the biometric subject looking at the device. The backward obstruction angle is calcula ted using the outermost edges of the back wall in the area 20 cm below and above the height of the "Frankfurt Horizon" of the test subject. 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The entire face is visible on the image, in the required resolution. 

- The IUTs quality assessment of the captured image is positive. 

- The calculated frontal obstruction angle _¯_ 1 is less than or equal to the maxi mum acceptable angle. 

- The calculated backward obstruction angle _¯_ 2 is less than or equal to the maximum acceptable angle. 

3 

_Description:_ 

- Position the test subject looking frontally into the camera at a distance of 70 cm from the camera system. This distance is to be understood as the length of the optical path. 

- Position the test subject with a body height of 200 cm in front of the camera system. Capture a facial image. 

- Calculate the frontal obstruction angle _¯_ 1 at the nasion of the biometric subject looking at the device. The frontal obstruction angle is calculated using the outermost edges of the device in the area 20 cm below and above the height of the "Frankfurt Horizon" of the test subject. 

- Calculate the backward obstruction angle _¯_ 2 at the nasion of the biometric subject looking at the device. The backward obstruction angle is calcula ted using the outermost edges of the back wall in the area 20 cm below and above the height of the "Frankfurt Horizon" of the test subject. 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The entire face is visible on the image, in the required resolution. 

- The IUTs quality assessment of the captured image is positive. 

- The calculated frontal obstruction angle _¯_ 1 is less than or equal to the maxi mum acceptable angle. 

- The calculated backward obstruction angle _¯_ 2 is less than or equal to the maximum acceptable angle.

## 2.8 Test Cases User Interface 

## 2.8.1 Test Cases FM UI-FI-BSJ 

**Test Case ID: TC-UI-FI-BSJ-001** _Scope_ Note that the requirement of presence of a feedback screen depends on the application context. If the acquisition system is required to have a feedback screen for the facial image acquisition wit hin the specific application context in question, or if the vendor decided to implement a feedback screen although it is not mandatory in the specific application context in question, the feedback screen shall be examined according to the following specifications: Examination of availability of feedback screen, digital mirror or physical mirror image and user guidance in the user interface shown to the biometric subject during facial image acquisition. _Preconditions_ **•** The IUT is in operation, required modules are loaded. **•** The integrated camera system used in the specific application scenario is connected. **•** The IUT implements a user interface of facial image acquisition shown to the biometric subject. **•** The IUT is able to display a digital mirror or physical mirror image and information for gui dance to help the biometric subject with the correct positioning. **•** The acquisition system is required by another Function Module to have a feedback screen for the facial acquisition **•** Fake facial images (e.g. created with a 3D-printer) are available as necessary test resource. _CTS Mode_ Not supported _Description_ **Step Description / Expected Result** 1 _Description:_ A biometric subject performs a compliant face presentation in the IUT's captu re area. _Expected Result:_ **•** A digital mirror or physical mirror image is shown by the IUT for guidance to help the biometric subject with the correct positioning. **•** A compliant facial image is acquired. 2 _Description:_ A biometric subject performs a non-compliant face presentation in the IUT's capture area where positional requirements are not met. _Expected Result:_ 

- A digital mirror or physical mirror image is shown by the IUT for guidance to help the biometric subject with the correct positioning. 

- A digital mirror or physical mirror image is shown by the IUT for guidance to help the biometric subject with the correct positioning. 

- The IUT displays user guidance information to help the biometric subject with the correct positioning in front of the camera. 

- The facial image acquisition is ommitted by the IUT. 

3 _Description:_ 

- A biometric subject initially performs a non-compliant face presentation in the IUT's capture area where positional requirements are not met. 

- The biometric subject follows the user guidance information provided by the IUT until a compliant face presentation is performed where positional requirements are met.

Initiate test case calling the IUT via the test interface 

_Expected Result:_ 

- Any relevant quality components is displayed in the GUI (at least one resul ting in NOK) 

- The overall quality is indicated visually by the IUT (NOK) 

- The cause for the negative overall quality assessment is displayed 

- The IUT provides the option to put in veto before release of the image (veto is put in via UI, i.e. image is accepted despite negative QA result computed by IUT ) 

- The IUT provides a warning message to the operator if PAD has been per formed and a presentation attack alarm has been raised. ## **Test Case ID: TC-CMP-FI-VER-001** 

1 

_Description:_ 

- Examine the documentation provided by the vendor of the comparison al gorithm. 

- _Expected Result:_ 

- The documentation gives information (typically a DET curve) of the perfor mance of the algorithm based on a reference database. 

- **•** The documentation gives evidence that the comparison algorithm ensures a working point of a maximum false-match-rate (FMR) of 0.1 % and a fal se-non-match-rate (FNMR) below 2 %. Stronger settings are allowed (lower FMR and/or FNMR). 

- **•** The documentation gives evidence that the comparison algorithm can be calibrated for the security level set within this specific scenario of verificati on.

_Description_ 

- **Step Description / Expected Result** 1 _Description:_ **•** Initiate test case by calling the IUT via the test interface. **•** Capture 10 fingerprints, each fingerprint plain and rolled (in identical, ma ted order) resulting in a total of 10 captured fingerprint pairs (plain, rolled) of matching fingerprints

- **•** The response contains information about both the `flat` and `roll` ed `Fin gerAcquisition` . 

- **•** The IUT returns a comparison score per pair of prints and each finger and a result of each verification (element `bio:Verification` ). 

- **•** Each single comparison score is above the threshold and each verification result is positive. 

- 2 _Description:_ **•** Initiate test case by calling the IUT via the test interface. 

   - Capture 10 fingerprints, each fingerprint plain and rolled (in identical, ma ted order) resulting in a total of 10 captured fingerprint pairs (plain, rolled) of matching fingerprints 

   - The IUT returns a comparison score per pair of prints and each finger and a result of each verification (element `bio:Verification` ). 

   - Capture 10 fingerprints, each fingerprint plain and rolled in non-mated or der (e.g. by permutating the fingers or using a second testperson) resulting in a total of 10 captured fingerprint pairs (plain, rolled) of non-matching fingerprints 

   - _Expected Result:_ 

   - The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

   - The response contains information about both the `flat` and `roll` ed `Fin gerAcquisition` . 

   - The IUT returns a comparison score per pair of prints and each finger and a result of each verification (element `bio:Verification` ). 

   - Each single comparison score is below the threshold and each verification result is negative.

## 2.10.1 Test Cases FM LOG-ALL-GENERIC 

|**Test Case ID:**|**TC-LOG-ALL-GENERIC-001**|**TC-LOG-ALL-GENERIC-001**|
|---|---|---|
|_Scope_|Examination of correct syntax and semantics for the logging of information regarding generic pro||
||cesses and transactions.||
|_Preconditions_|**•**|Five sets of logging data (encoded as XML) each, e.g. produced by performing five deviating pro|
|||cesses using IUT|
||**•**|The performed steps for each log data set are documented.|
||**•**|TR-03121 XML schema definition files and documentation.|
||**•**|Vendor specific documentation for error codes and further information.|
|_CTS Mode_|File based||
|_Description_|**Step**<br>**Description / Expected Result**||
||1|_Description:_|
|||Validate each log file against the TR-03121 schema definition_"trbio6v1.xsd"_.|
|||_Expected Result:_|
|||Each file passes the validation process without errors.|
||2|_Description:_|
|||Verify the content of each log file and check the contained information.|



Federal Office for Information Security 

116 

2 Test Cases Function Modules 

## **Test Case ID: TC-LOG-ALL-GENERIC-001** 

_Expected Result:_ 

- Each log file contains at least one element of type `type.transaction` . 

- The transaction UUIDs are globally unique. 

- The value of `Location` is compliant to the semantic of the relevant profile. 

- If this transaction is dependent or derived from another transaction, the `TransactionReference` is set to the corresponding transaction UUID. 

- In case of any abnormal termination of the transaction or any of its subprocesses, the `ErrorCode` is set. 

- Each during the transaction performed enrolment process is represented by an `Enrolment` element. 

   - If a central system replies directly with enrolment statues information, the `SubmitTime` is set. 

   - A `ControlVerification` element is logged for any control verification performed during the enrolment process. 

- Each during the transaction performed identification process is represented by an `Identification` element. 

If a central system provides detailed scoring information, the list of candi dates is contained. 

- Each during the transaction performed verification process is represented by a `Verification` element. 

   - All performed comparisons are logged. 

   - For each comparison the vendor specific score as well as the threshold are logged.

The output image fulfills the requirements to be sent to the document production (e.g. `FaceAcquisition/Records/Bi naryRecord/@type` or `FaceDelivery/Records/BinaryRecord/@type` is jpeg2000). All other binary data values of face deliveries or acquisitions have been deleted. However, multiple appearances of these elements are permit ted before being fed to the selection procedure (e.g. from any acquisition or delivery application).

## **Test Case ID: TC-LOG-FP-GID-001** 

_Expected Result:_ 

- If the XML-element `FingerprintCaptureAllowed` is true the XML-ele ment `FingerAcquisition` occurs at least once for applicants applying for: 

   - Resindence Permit 

   - Passport 

   - Identity Card 

- The XML-element `FingerAcquisition` does not occur if the XML-element `FingerprintCaptureAllowed` is false. 

- The XML-elements of `FingerAcquisition/Records/BinaryRecord` may be missing e.g. if fingers are physically impossible to acquire. 

- The XML-element `FingerAcquisition/Records` occurs at maximum once in one of the FingerAcquisstion XML-elements. 

- The XML-element `FingerAcquisition/Records/XMLRecord` is not used. 

- The XML-element `FingerAcquisition/Records/BinaryRecord/Bina ryData/Value` occurs at least once and at maximum two times. 

- The XML-element `FingerAcquisition/Records/BinaryRecord/@type` is icao-cbeff-bit-bdb-19794-4 

- The XML-element `FingerAcquisition/Records/BinaryRecord/Data` occurs exactly once per record.

## 2.11 Test Cases Coding 

## 2.11.1 Test Cases FM COD-ALL-ARE 

## **Test Case ID: TC-COD-ALL-ARE-001** 

_Scope_ Examine proper overall encoding of input data (considering biographic, biometric and transaction specific data) for identification purposes. 

- _Preconditions_ **•** The IUT is in operation, required modules are loaded. 

   - The hardware used in the specific application scenario is connected 

   - The multi fingerprint scanner used in the specific application scenario is connected 

   - IUT is configured to perform the identification process including the manual acquisition of ten plain fingerprints using the IUT. 

   - The IUT is able to return the results for every captured image via the test interface. 

   - The following test resources are necessary: 

      - One test subject is available to perform the acquisition with. 

      - Provision of any other than biometric data (e.g. biographic, agency or process related) may be done in form of static dummy values by IUT, so that the process can be finalized and the re sulting data is returned by IUT as GSAT3 XML. 

- _CTS Mode_ **•** interactive 

   - no provision of pre-defined input data 

   - `HTTP method: GET` 

   - test case variants: 

```
/TR03122/TC-COD-ALL-ARE-001/1
```

**Table 2.180** TC-COD-ALL-ARE-001 Test Case Variants _Description_ **Step Description / Expected Result** 

Federal Office for Information Security 

127 

2 Test Cases Function Modules 

## **Test Case ID: TC-COD-ALL-ARE-001** 

1 

_Description:_ 

- Initiate test case by calling the IUT via the test interface. 

- Perform the whole acquisition process using the IUT in the identification scenario with subsequent examination of returned result data 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file are6v1.xsd. 

- The response contains the correct number of elements based on the XPath expression `/are:are-log/bio:Records/bio:XMLRecord[@type='g sat-xml']/tradd:XMLData/tradd:Value` . Exactly 1 element is expected in accordance to the acquistion process. 

- The XML embedded in the element "/are:are-log/bio:Records/bio:XMLRe cord[@type='gsat-xml']/tradd:XMLData/tradd:Value" of the response XML is GSAT3-xml. 

**Table 2.179** Test Case ID: TC-COD-ALL-ARE-001 

## 2.11.2 Test Cases FM COD-ALL-BCL 

## **Test Case ID: TC-COD-ALL-BCL-001** 

_Scope_ Examine requirements and interfaces for the overall coding of biometric (facial image and finger prints) and biographic data used within the context of border control. _Preconditions_ **•** The IUT is in operation, required modules are loaded. 

   - The multi fingerprint scanner used in the specific application scenario is connected 

   - The hardware used in the specific application scenario is connected 

   - The IUT implements an interface for conformance testing where biometric data can be acqui red. 

   - The following test resources are necessary: **•** One test subject is available to perform the acquisition with.

|_Description_|**Step**<br>**Description / Expected Result**|||



Federal Office for Information Security 

134 

2 Test Cases Function Modules 

## **Test Case ID: TC-COD-FP-GID-001** 

1 

_Description:_ 

Decoding of the CBEFF Header, Biometric Header Template (BHT) (XML ele ment <data>) for each fingerprint 

_Expected Result:_ 

Provision of the according biometric container including the fingerprint data: 

- CBEFF Patron Header Version = '0101' 

- BDB Biometric Type: value = '08' (Fingerprint) 

- BDB Biometric Subtype: one of the following values: 

- 0000 0101 (thumb right) 

- 0000 0110 (thumb left) 

- 0000 1001 (index right) 

- 0000 1010 (index left) 

- 0000 1101 (middle finger right) 

- 0000 1110 (middle finger left) 

- 0001 0001 (ring finger right) 

- 0001 0010 (ring finger left) 

- BDB Format Owner: value = '0x0101' (ISO/IEC JTC 1 SC 37-Biometrics) 

- BDB Format Type: value = '0x0007' (ISO/IEC JTC 1 SC 37-Biometrics) 

2 _Description:_ 

Decoding of the general record header 

_Expected Result:_ 

- Format Identifier = 0x46495200 (“FIR” - Finger Image Record) 

- Version Number = 0x30313000 (“010”) 

- Record Length = 32+ 1 * (14 bytes + Data length) 

- Capture device ID = 2 bytes (Vendor specified) 

- Image acquisition level = '0x001F' (Level 31) or '0x0029' (Level 41) 

- Number of fingers = 1 

- Scale units = 1 (pixel/inch) 

- Scan resolution (horiz) = 01F4 (500ppi) 

- Scan resolution (vert) = 01F4 (500ppi) 

- Image resolution (horiz) <= Scan resolution (horiz) 

- Image resolution (vert) <= Scan resolution (vert) 

- Pixel depth = 08 (256 grae levels) 

- Image compression Algorithm = 02 (WSQ) 

- Reserved = 00 00 

3 _Description:_ 

Decoding of the finger image header record 

Federal Office for Information Security 

135 

2 Test Cases Function Modules 

## **Test Case ID: TC-COD-FP-GID-001** 

_Expected Result:_ 

   - Length of finger data block (bytes) = includes header and the image data block 

   - Finger position = finger code {1,2,3,4,6,7,8,9} 

   - Count of views = '01' 

   - View number = '01' 

   - Finger image quality {0-100} or 255 in case of error 

   - Impression type = 0 (Live-scan plain) 

   - Horizontal line length >0 

   - Vertical line length >0 

- Reserved = 00 

- 4 _Description:_ Examination of the finger image data _Expected Result:_ 

   - Finger image data size < 18432 bytes 

   - Encoded as WSQ image 

   - `sf` value coded in the image header unequal 0 

**Table 2.194** Test Case ID: TC-COD-FP-GID-001

|_Scope_|Examination of the availability and the correctness of veto power of the operator in the automated||
||and manual capturing process for facial image acquisition.||
|_Applicability_|Note that the TC is separated in different groups of steps: Steps 1-4 test the manual rejection suc||
||ceeded by acceptance during automated facial image acquisition. Steps 5-11 test manual rejection||
||succeeded by acceptance, including switching from manual to automated facial image acquisition.||
||Note that the support of a manual service mode is optional within the application context German||
||Identity Documents (GID). If the IUT does not supports a manual mode, the second group of steps||
||are omitted.||
|_Preconditions_|**•**<br>The IUT is in operation, required modules are loaded.||
||**•**<br>The integrated camera system used in the specific application scenario is connected||
||**•**<br>If HLBS is used by the system, the HLBS service definition for "Facial Image Acquisition" speci||
||fied in TR-03121-2.2 is implemented.||
||**•**<br>The following test resources are necessary:||

||**•**<br>Documentation of the configured timeout is at hand.||

||**•**<br>no provision of pre-defined input data||

- **Step Description / Expected Result** 1 _Description:_ **•** Initiate test case by calling the IUT via the test interface. **•** A single biometric subject is present in the IUT's capture area. **•** The operator starts an automated acquisition process of a facial image for enrolment or verification/identification. 

- **•** The operator starts the capturing process of a facial image. _Expected Result:_ **•** The IUT executes the process autonomously, i.e. the system handles the ent ire process execution, including configuration of the camera system to the height of the biometric subject. 

- **•** An image is captured and shown to the Operator. Corresponding QA results are shown. 

- 2 _Description:_ 

   - The operator conducts a manual QA of the acquired facial image and re views the software-based QA result. The operator rejects the acquired facial image.

- 3 _Description:_ **•** The operator starts the capturing process of a facial image. _Expected Result:_

## **Test Case ID: TC-PAP-ACQ-FI-AUTO-1-001** 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The IUT captures a face image. 

- Depending on the PAD configuration, the IUT performs a PAD. In this case, all acceptance conditions are met according to FM PAD-FI-APP. 

- The IUT assesses the quality of the captured face image: The acceptance conditions are not met according to the respective FM QA-FI-*. 

- The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

- The response contains information regarding the process conduction as well as the encoded facial image ( `bio:FaceAcquisition` ). 

- Within the application context Border Control (BCL), the time required for the overall facial image acquisition process did not exceed ten seconds if PAD is performed or seven seconds if no PAD is performed. 

- Within the application context German Identity Documents (GID), the time required for the overall facial image acquisition process did not exceed thir ty seconds (including PAD and, if applicable, excluding background replace ment).

## **Test Case ID: TC-PAP-ACQ-FP2P-SV-1-001** 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The acquisition is triggered by placing the finger(s) on the acquisition hard ware in the two finger mode (right index and left index). 

- The IUT detects the insufficient quality and restarts the acquisition of the respective finger. 

- The acquisition of the slap of insufficient fingerprint quality is repeated twice (i.e. total of three performed attempts). 

- After the third attempt the IUT switches to the acquisition of the thumb of the respective hand. 

- The IUT finishes without any error message (apart from the bad quality). 

- The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

- The response contains an element `//bio:FingerAcquisition` and rele vant sub-elements containing the information corresponding to the perfor med acquisition process. 

- If insufficient quality was enforced for the left index finger: PAD has been performed for the fingerprint images of right and left index fingers and left thumb. 

- If insufficient quality was enforced for the right index finger: PAD has been performed for the fingerprint images of right index finger and thumb and left index finger. 

- The PAD system didn't report an error. 

- The PAD system did report no warning of an attack. 

- No Uniqueness check error or warning was reported. 

- QA values have been calculated for all fingerprint images individually. 

- If insufficient quality was enforced for the left index finger: The response contains elements `//bio:Records/bio:BinaryRecord/tradd:Binary Data/tradd:Value` containing base64 encoded fingerprint images of the right index and left thumb. 

- If insufficient quality was enforced for the right index finger: The response contains elements `//bio:Records/bio:BinaryRecord/tradd:Binary Data/tradd:Value` containing base64 encoded fingerprint images of the right thumb and left index. 

- The images contained in the response correspond to the fingers, that were captured.

1 

_Description:_ 

- Initiate test case by calling the IUT via the test interface. 

- As the biometric subject: Present two index fingers simultaneously, but place the fingers in such a manner (with bad quality) that the pre-qualifica tion is insufficient. 

- As the operator: Force the capture of the two fingerprints manually. 

- Repeat the insufficient presentation and the forced capture twice (i.e. a total of three times). 

- As the operator: Accept the capture that is identified as the best by the IUT. 

- Examine the response returned by the IUT via the test interface. 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The acquisition starts by placing the finger(s) on the acquisition hardware in the two finger mode (right index and left index). 

- The acquisition is triggered not until the operator forced the capture manu ally. 

- The capture is repeated twice before the IUT gives the option to accept one of the slaps.

- The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

- The response contains an element `//bio:FingerAcquisition` and rele vant sub-elements containing the information corresponding to the perfor med acquisition process of the index finger slap. 

- PAD has been performed for both fingerprint images. 

- No Uniqueness check error or warning was reported. 

- For each capture attempt: QA values have been calculated for both finger print images individually. 

- For each capture attempt: QA values are below the defined threshold. 

- The IUT selected the fingerprint capture attempt with the best QA-score (via `@selected` , confirmed by the operator `@veto` ). 

- The response contains elements `//bio:Records/bio:BinaryRe cord/tradd:BinaryData/tradd:Value` containing base64 encoded fin gerprint images of the right and left index fingers. 

- The images contained in the response correspond to the fingers, that were captured and selected.

- If insufficient quality was enforced for the right index finger: The response contains elements `//bio:Records/bio:BinaryRecord/tradd:Binary Data/tradd:Value` containing base64 encoded fingerprint images of the right thumb and left index.

## 3.10 Test Cases PAP ACQ-FP10R-SV-1: Ten Finger Rolled Supervised Acquisition for Enrolment 

- **Test Case ID: TC-PAP-ACQ-FP10R-SV-1-001** _Scope_ Examination of the correct process for the acquisition of up to ten rolled fingerprints. _Preconditions_ **•** The IUT is in operation, required modules are loaded. **•** The fingerprint scanner providing rolled fingerprints used in the specific application scenario is connected 

- **•** If HLBS is used by the system, the HLBS service definition for "Rolled Fingerprint Acquisition" specified in TR-03121-2.2 is implemented. 

- **•** The following test resources are necessary: **•** One test subject is available to perform the acquisition with. **•** Product documentation of IUT is at hand (e.g. data sheet, manual). 

- _CTS Mode_ **•** interactive **•** no provision of pre-defined input data **•** `HTTP method: GET` **•** test case variants: `/TR03122/TC-PAP-ACQ-FP10R-SV-1-001/1` 

**Table 3.38** TC-PAP-ACQ-FP10R-SV-1-001 Test Case Variants 

_Description_ **Step Description / Expected Result** 1 _Description:_ **•** Initiate test case by calling the IUT via the test interface. 

- Perform the flat acquisition process of all fingers in order to obtain the ne cessary reference data for the control verification. Ensure sufficient quality and correct execution throughout the process. 

- Perform the rolled acquisition process of all fingers, beginning with the thumb of the right hand and ending with the little finger of the left hand. Ensure sufficient quality and correct execution throughout the process. 

- Examine the response returned by the IUT via the test interface. 

- _Expected Result:_ 

- The acquisition of the reference slaps is performed by the IUT. 

- The IUT successively performs the acquisition of the rolled fingerprints. 

- The IUT finishes without any error message. 

- The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

- The response includes the information corresponding to the performed ac quisition process ( `bio:FingerAcquisition` and relevant sub-elements). The raw fingerprint data may be stored either as sub-element of this ele ment or as descendant of the sibling element `bio:Records` 

- The images contained in the response correspond to the fingers, that were captured.

- Perform the rolled acquisition process of all fingers, beginning with the thumb of the right hand and ending with the little finger of the left hand. Enforce a hardware reported issue during the rolled capture process for at least one finger . Consult the documentation of the hardware in regards of supported issues (e.g. “finger shifted”). 

- If a hardware reported issue was induced for a capture: Repeat the capture of that finger enforcing bad quality. 

- For each finger, where no hardware-reported issue was induced: Capture this finger enforcing good quality. 

- If bad quality was enforced during a capture: Repeat the capture of that fin ger enforcing good quality. 

- Examine the response returned by the IUT via the test interface. 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The acquisition of the reference slaps is performed by the IUT. 

- The IUT successively performs the acquisition of the rolled fingerprints. 

- If a hardware issue was induced: The IUT detects the hardware issue and re starts the acquisition of the current finger. 

- If insufficient quality was enforced: The IUT detects insufficient quality and restarts the acquisition of the current finger. 

- If a hardware issue was induced: The acquisition of that finger is repeated twice in total (one time due to the hardware issue, one time due to the quali ty). 

- The IUT finishes without any error message that is disrupting the flow of the PAP or that is unrelated to the induced hardware issue or insufficient quality. 

- The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

- The response includes the information corresponding to the performed ac quisition process (elements `bio:FingerAcquisition` and relevant sub-ele ments). The raw fingerprint data may be stored either as sub-element of this element or as descendant of the sibling element `bio:Records` . 

- The IUT selected the fingerprint capture attempts with the best QA-score (via `@selected` ). 

- The `rejectionReason` element matches the reason chosen by the operator 

- The images contained in the response correspond to the fingers, that were captured.

3 Test Cases Partial Application Processes 

## **Test Case ID: TC-PAP-ACQ-FP10R-SV-1-002** 

3 

_Description:_ 

- Initiate test case by calling the IUT via the test interface. 

- Perform the flat/control acquisition process of all fingers in order to obtain the necessary reference data for the control verification. Ensure sufficient quality and correct execution throughout the process. Do not induce any er rors. 

- Perform the rolled acquisition process of all fingers, beginning with the thumb of the right hand and ending with the little finger of the left hand. Enforce a hardware reported issue during the rolled capture process for at least one finger . Consult the documentation of the hardware in regards of supported issues (e.g. “finger shifted”). 

- If a hardware reported issue was induced for a capture: Repeat the capture of that finger enforcing bad quality. 

- If bad quality was enforced during a capture: Repeat the capture of that fin ger inducing a hardware reported issue. 

- For the remaining fingers: Capture this finger enforcing good quality and no hardware reported issues. 

- Examine the response returned by the IUT via the test interface. 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The acquisition of the reference slaps is performed by the IUT. 

- The IUT successively performs the acquisition of the rolled fingerprints. 

- If a hardware issue was induced: The IUT detects the hardware issue and re starts the acquisition of the current finger. 

- If insufficient quality was enforced: The IUT detects insufficient quality and restarts the acquisition of the current finger. 

- If a hardware issue was induced on the third capture of a finger: The IUT de tects the hardware issue and offers to either restart the capture or to conti nue with the current result. 

- The IUT finishes without any error message that is disrupting the flow of the PAP or that is unrelated to the induced hardware issue or insufficient quality. 

- The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

- The response includes the information corresponding to the performed ac quisition process (elements `bio:FingerAcquisition` and relevant sub-ele ments). The raw fingerprint data may be stored either as sub-element of this element or as descendant of the sibling element `bio:Records` . 

- The first repetition with insufficient QA score is marked as `@selected` . 

- The `@rejectionReason` element matches the reason chosen by the opera tor. 

- The images contained in the response correspond to the fingers, that were captured.

## **Test Case ID: TC-PAP-ACQ-FP2P-USV-1-001** 

- _Scope_ Verify the correct acquisition process for a slap consisting of two fingers: Acquisitions are made compliantly, with qa-thresholds set too high, no fingers presented at all and an induced sequence error. 

- _Preconditions_ **•** The IUT is in operation, required modules are loaded. **•** The multi fingerprint scanner used in the specific application scenario is connected **•** If HLBS is used by the system, the HLBS service definition for "Fingerprint Acquisition" specified in TR-03121-2.2 is implemented. 

- **•** Possibility to configure minimium required quality thresholds **•** The following test resources are necessary: **•** One test subject is available to perform the acquisition with. **•** Product documentation of IUT is at hand (e.g. data sheet, manual). 

- _CTS Mode_ **•** interactive **•** no provision of pre-defined input data **•** `HTTP method: GET` **•** test case variants: `/TR03122/TC-PAP-ACQ-FP2P-USV-1-001/1`

   - Follow the instructions of the IUT for the biometric test subject regarding slap placement on the scanner. 

   - Examine the response returned by the IUT via the test interface. 

_Expected Result:_ 

- The IUT delivers a response that is conformant to the schema file trbio6v1.xsd. 

- The captured slap image is segmented into two single fingerprint images. 

- PAD has been performed for both fingerprint images. 

- The PAD system didn't report an error. 

- The PAD system did report no warning of an attack. 

- No Uniqueness check error or warning was reported. 

- QA values have been calculated for both fingerprint images individually. 

- The overall finger capture process was performed in under ten seconds. 

- The response contains two encoded fingerprint images for the index fingers. 

- The images contained in the response correspond to the fingers, that were captured.
